          <p>(3) obtain the nonsigning intervenor's signature within a reasonable time after an agreement was reached with the intervenor.</p>
          <p class="bl">The partial stipulation must include detailed and case-specific support for the parties' statements. In addition, the partial stipulation must reserve the nonsigning intervenor's interests to pursue its claim at a hearing on the merits, and must contain a statement that the employee will cooperate at the hearing.</p>
          <p>(b) Prior to filing the partial stipulation for approval, a copy of the partial stipulation must be served on all parties, including the nonsigning intervenor, together with a written notification that the settling parties intend to file the partial stipulation for approval by a compensation judge and of the nonsigning intervenor's right to request a hearing on the merits of the intervenor's claim.</p>
          <p>(c) Within ten days after service of a partial stipulation for settlement and notice of an intent to file for approval by a compensation judge, a nonsigning intervenor may serve and file a written objection to approval of the partial stipulation, which filing must provide a detailed and case-specific factual basis establishing that approval of the partial stipulation will adversely impact the rights of the intervenor.</p>
          <p>(d) After expiration of the ten-day period within which a nonsigning intervenor may serve and file its written objection, any party may file for approval a partial stipulation for settlement which conforms with this section. An affidavit of service must accompany the partial stipulation when it is filed for approval.</p>
          <p>(e) Unless the compensation judge has a reasonable belief that approval of the partial stipulation will adversely impact the rights of the nonsigning intervenor, the compensation judge shall immediately issue the award and file it with the commissioner. The issuance of the award shall be accompanied by notice to the intervenors and other parties of their right to request amended findings within a period of 30 days following the date of issuance in conformity with applicable law.</p>
          <p>(f) If the compensation judge has a reasonable belief that approval of the partial stipulation will adversely impact the rights of the intervenor, the compensation judge shall disapprove the stipulation by written order detailing a factual basis for the determination of adverse impact.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.176.521.3">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 3.<span class="headnote">Setting aside award upon settlement.</span></h2>
          <p>Notwithstanding the provisions of subdivision 1, 2, or 2a, or any provision in the agreement of settlement to the contrary, upon the filing of a petition by any party to the settlement, the Workers' Compensation Court of Appeals may set aside an award made upon a settlement, pursuant to this chapter. In appropriate cases, the Workers' Compensation Court of Appeals may refer the matter to the chief administrative law judge for assignment to a compensation judge for hearing.</p>
        </div>
      </div>
      <div class="history" id="stat.176.521.history
			">
        <h2>History: </h2>
        <p class="first"><a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1953/0/755/#laws.0.69.0">1953 c 755 s 69</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1973/0/388/#laws.0.127.0">1973 c 388 s 127</a>,128; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1975/0/271/#laws.0.6.0">1975 c 271 s 6</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1975/0/359/#laws.0.23.0">1975 c 359 s 23</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1976/0/134/#laws.0.78.0">1976 c 134 s 78</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1979/0/271/#laws.0.1.0">1979 c 271 s 1</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1979/1/3/#laws.0.60.0">Ex1979 c 3 s 60</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1981/0/346/#laws.0.134.0">1981 c 346 s 134</a>,135; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1981/3/2/#laws.1.24.0">3Sp1981 c 2 art 1 s 24</a>-26; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1983/0/290/#laws.0.156.0">1983 c 290 s 156</a>-158; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1984/0/640/#laws.0.32.0">1984 c 640 s 32</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1986/0/444/#laws.0.0.0">1986 c 444</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1986/0/461/#laws.0.33.0">1986 c 461 s 33</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1987/0/332/#laws.0.98.0">1987 c 332 s 98</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1993/0/194/#laws.0.7.0">1993 c 194 s 7</a>,8; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2013/0/70/#laws.1.7.0">2013 c 70 art 1 s 7</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2017/0/94/#laws.5.3.0">2017 c 94 art 5 s 3</a></p>
      </div>
      <div class="section" id="stat.176.522">
        <h1 class="shn">176.522 NOTICE TO EMPLOYER.</h1>
        <p>An employer shall be notified by the insurer 30 days after any final valid settlement is approved or otherwise made final under any provision of this chapter. The notice shall include all terms of the settlement including the total amount of money required to be reserved in order to pay the claim.</p>
      </div>
      <div class="history" id="stat.176.522.history
			">
        <h2>History: </h2>
        <p class="first">
          <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1983/0/290/#laws.0.159.0">1983 c 290 s 159</a>
        </p>
      </div>
      <div class="sr" id="stat.176.53"><b>176.53</b> [Repealed, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1953/0/755/#laws.0.83.0">1953 c 755 s 83</a>]</div>
      <h1 class="fgh">GOVERNMENT ISSUES</h1>
      <div class="section" id="stat.176.531">
        <h1 class="shn">176.531 AWARD OF COMPENSATION AGAINST A POLITICAL SUBDIVISION OR SCHOOL DISTRICT.</h1>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.176.531.1">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subdivision 1.<span class="headnote">Preferred claim.</span></h2>
          <p>Where there has been an award of compensation under this chapter to be paid by a political subdivision or a school district, the entitlement of a person to payment under the award is a preferred claim against the subdivision or district. The award shall be paid when and as ordered from the general fund of the subdivision or district, and from the current tax apportionment received by the subdivision or district for the credit of the general fund.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.176.531.2">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 2.<span class="headnote">Payment from general fund.</span></h2>
          <p>When the political subdivision or school district has issued an order or warrant for payment of compensation, and the order or warrant has not been paid, it is a preferred claim which shall be paid from the general fund and from current tax apportionments received for the credit of the general fund before any subsequent claim for compensation is paid.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.176.531.3">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 3.<span class="headnote">Prompt payment.</span></h2>
          <p>It is the intent of this section that there be prompt payment of compensation.</p>
        </div>
      </div>
      <div class="history" id="stat.176.531.history
			">
        <h2>History: </h2>
        <p class="first"><a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1953/0/755/#laws.0.70.0">1953 c 755 s 70</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1973/0/388/#laws.0.129.0">1973 c 388 s 129</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1981/0/346/#laws.0.136.0">1981 c 346 s 136</a></p>
      </div>
      <div class="sr" id="stat.176.54"><b>176.54</b> [Repealed, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1953/0/755/#laws.0.83.0">1953 c 755 s 83</a>]</div>
      <div class="sr" id="stat.176.540"><b>176.540</b> MS 1992 [Renumbered <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/176.5401">176.5401</a>] </div>
      <div class="sr" id="stat.176.5401"><b>176.5401</b> [Repealed, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2008/0/204/#laws.0.43.0">2008 c 204 s 43</a>]</div>
      <div class="section" id="stat.176.541">
        <h1 class="shn">176.541 STATE DEPARTMENTS.</h1>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.176.541.1">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subdivision 1.<span class="headnote">Application of chapter to state employees.</span></h2>
          <p>This chapter applies to the employees of any department of this state as defined in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/3.732#stat.3.732.1">3.732, subdivision 1</a>, clause (1).</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.176.541.2">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 2.<span class="headnote">Defense of claim against state.</span></h2>
          <p>When the commissioner of administration believes that a claim against the state for compensation should be contested, the commissioner shall defend the state claim.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.176.541.3">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 3.<span class="headnote">Duties of attorney general.</span></h2>
          <p>At any stage in such a compensation proceeding, the attorney general may assume the duty of defending the state. When the commissioner of administration or a department of this state requests the attorney general to assume the defense, the attorney general shall do so.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.176.541.4">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 4.<span class="headnote">Medical examination of employee; witnesses; conduct of defense.</span></h2>
          <p>In conducting a defense against a claim for compensation, the commissioner of administration or the attorney general, as the case may be, may require that an employee submit to a medical examination, procure the attendance of expert and other witnesses at a hearing, and do any other act necessary to conduct a proper defense.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.176.541.5">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 5.<span class="headnote">Expenses of conducting defense.</span></h2>
          <p>The expenses of conducting a defense shall be charged to the department which employs the employee involved. These expenses shall be paid from the state compensation revolving fund.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.176.541.6">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 6.<span class="headnote">Legal and clerical help.</span></h2>
          <p>The commissioner of administration may employ legal and clerical help. The salaries of these persons shall be paid from the state compensation revolving fund, but shall be apportioned among the several departments of the state in relation to the amount of compensation paid to employees of any department as against the total amount of compensation paid to employees of all departments.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.176.541.7">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 7.</h2>
          <p>[Repealed, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2017/0/94/#laws.3.10.0">2017 c 94 art 3 s 10</a>]</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.176.541.7a">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 7a.<span class="headnote">Exceptions.</span></h2>
          <p>This section does not apply to the University of Minnesota.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.176.541.8">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 8.<span class="headnote">State may insure.</span></h2>
          <p>The state of Minnesota may elect to insure its liability under the workers' compensation law for persons employed under the federal Workforce Innovation and Opportunity Act, and similar programs, with an insurer properly licensed in Minnesota.</p>
        </div>
      </div>
      <div class="history" id="stat.176.541.history
			">
        <h2>History: </h2>
        <p class="first"><a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1953/0/755/#laws.0.71.0">1953 c 755 s 71</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1967/0/8/#laws.0.1.0">1967 c 8 s 1</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1971/0/422/#laws.0.10.0">1971 c 422 s 10</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1973/0/388/#laws.0.130.0">1973 c 388 s 130</a>-133; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1975/0/2/#laws.0.2.0">1975 c 2 s 2</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1975/0/359/#laws.0.23.0">1975 c 359 s 23</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1986/0/444/#laws.0.0.0">1986 c 444</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1987/0/332/#laws.0.100.0">1987 c 332 s 100</a>-103; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2008/0/204/#laws.0.28.0">2008 c 204 s 28</a>-31; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2017/0/94/#laws.3.6.0">2017 c 94 art 3 s 6</a>-8</p>
      </div>
      <div class="sr" id="stat.176.55"><b>176.55</b> [Repealed, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1953/0/755/#laws.0.83.0">1953 c 755 s 83</a>]</div>
      <div class="section" id="stat.176.551">
        <h1 class="shn">176.551 REPORTS.</h1>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.176.551.1">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subdivision 1.<span class="headnote">Heads of state departments to report accidents to employees.</span></h2>
          <p>Except as provided in subdivision 2, the head of a department of the state shall report each accident which occurs to an employee as and in the manner required by this chapter.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.176.551.2">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 2.<span class="headnote">Contents.</span></h2>
          <p>The report need not contain a statement relating to liability to pay compensation as required by this chapter.</p>
        </div>
      </div>
      <div class="history" id="stat.176.551.history
			">
        <h2>History: </h2>
        <p class="first">
          <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1953/0/755/#laws.0.72.0">1953 c 755 s 72</a>
        </p>
      </div>
      <div class="sr" id="stat.176.56"><b>176.56</b> [Repealed, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1953/0/755/#laws.0.83.0">1953 c 755 s 83</a>]</div>
      <div class="section" id="stat.176.561">
        <h1 class="shn">176.561 WORKERS' COMPENSATION COURT OF APPEALS POWERS AND DUTIES AS TO STATE EMPLOYEES; PROCEDURE FOR DETERMINING LIABILITY.</h1>
        <p>The division, a compensation judge and the Workers' Compensation Court of Appeals have the same powers and duties in matters relating to state employees as they have in relation to other employees.</p>
        <p>Except as specifically provided otherwise in this chapter, the procedure for determining the liability of the state for compensation is the same as that applicable in other cases.</p>
      </div>
      <div class="history" id="stat.176.561.history
			">
        <h2>History: </h2>
        <p class="first"><a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1953/0/755/#laws.0.73.0">1953 c 755 s 73</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1973/0/388/#laws.0.134.0">1973 c 388 s 134</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1975/0/271/#laws.0.6.0">1975 c 271 s 6</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1975/0/359/#laws.0.23.0">1975 c 359 s 23</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1976/0/134/#laws.0.78.0">1976 c 134 s 78</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1983/0/290/#laws.0.160.0">1983 c 290 s 160</a></p>
      </div>
      <div class="sr" id="stat.176.57"><b>176.57</b> [Repealed, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1953/0/755/#laws.0.83.0">1953 c 755 s 83</a>]</div>
      <div class="section" id="stat.176.571">
        <h1 class="shn">176.571 INVESTIGATIONS OF INJURIES TO STATE EMPLOYEES.</h1>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.176.571.1">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subdivision 1.<span class="headnote">Preliminary investigation.</span></h2>
          <p>When the head of a department has filed a report or the commissioner of administration has otherwise received information of the occurrence of an injury to a state employee for which liability to pay compensation may exist, the commissioner of administration shall make a preliminary investigation to determine the question of probable liability.</p>
          <p>In making this investigation, the commissioner of administration may require the assistance of the head of any department or any employee of the state. The commissioner of administration may require that all facts be furnished which appear in the records of any state department bearing on the issue.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.176.571.2">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 2.<span class="headnote">Determination by department.</span></h2>
          <p>When the commissioner of administration has completed an investigation, the commissioner shall inform the claimant, the head of the employing department, and the commissioner of management and budget in writing of the action taken.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.176.571.3">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 3.</h2>
          <p>[Repealed, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1987/0/332/#laws.0.117.0">1987 c 332 s 117</a>]</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.176.571.4">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 4.</h2>
          <p>[Repealed, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1987/0/332/#laws.0.117.0">1987 c 332 s 117</a>]</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.176.571.5">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 5.</h2>
          <p>[Repealed, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1987/0/332/#laws.0.117.0">1987 c 332 s 117</a>]</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.176.571.6">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 6.</h2>
          <p>[Repealed, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1987/0/332/#laws.0.117.0">1987 c 332 s 117</a>]</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.176.571.7">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 7.</h2>
          <p>[Repealed, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1987/0/332/#laws.0.117.0">1987 c 332 s 117</a>]</p>
        </div>
      </div>
      <div class="history" id="stat.176.571.history
			">
        <h2>History: </h2>
        <p class="first"><a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1953/0/755/#laws.0.74.0">1953 c 755 s 74</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1973/0/388/#laws.0.135.0">1973 c 388 s 135</a>-141; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1983/0/290/#laws.0.161.0">1983 c 290 s 161</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1984/0/640/#laws.0.32.0">1984 c 640 s 32</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1986/0/444/#laws.0.0.0">1986 c 444</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1987/0/332/#laws.0.104.0">1987 c 332 s 104</a>,105; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2008/0/204/#laws.0.32.0">2008 c 204 s 32</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2009/0/101/#laws.2.70.0">2009 c 101 art 2 s 70</a>,109; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2016/0/110/#laws.2.3.0">2016 c 110 art 2 s 3</a></p>
      </div>
      <div class="section" id="stat.176.572">
        <h1 class="shn">176.572 CONTRACT WITH INSURANCE CARRIERS.</h1>
        <p>The commissioner of administration may contract with group health insurance carriers or health maintenance organizations to provide health care services and reimburse health care payments for injured state employees entitled to benefits under this chapter.</p>
      </div>
      <div class="history" id="stat.176.572.history
			">
        <h2>History: </h2>
        <p class="first"><a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1983/0/290/#laws.0.162.0">1983 c 290 s 162</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1987/0/332/#laws.0.106.0">1987 c 332 s 106</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2008/0/204/#laws.0.33.0">2008 c 204 s 33</a></p>
      </div>
      <div class="sr" id="stat.176.58"><b>176.58</b> [Repealed, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1953/0/755/#laws.0.83.0">1953 c 755 s 83</a>]</div>
      <div class="section" id="stat.176.581">
        <h1 class="shn">176.581 PAYMENT TO STATE EMPLOYEES.</h1>
        <p>Upon a request prepared by the commissioner of administration, and in accordance with the terms of the order awarding compensation, the commissioner of management and budget shall pay compensation to the employee or the employee's dependent. These payments shall be made from money appropriated for this purpose.</p>
      </div>
      <div class="history" id="stat.176.581.history
			">
        <h2>History: </h2>
        <p class="first"><a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1953/0/755/#laws.0.75.0">1953 c 755 s 75</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1973/0/388/#laws.0.142.0">1973 c 388 s 142</a>-144; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1973/0/492/#laws.0.14.0">1973 c 492 s 14</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1986/0/444/#laws.0.0.0">1986 c 444</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1987/0/332/#laws.0.107.0">1987 c 332 s 107</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2003/0/112/#laws.2.26.0">2003 c 112 art 2 s 26</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2008/0/204/#laws.0.34.0">2008 c 204 s 34</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2009/0/101/#laws.2.109.0">2009 c 101 art 2 s 109</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2019/1/10/#laws.3.26.0">1Sp2019 c 10 art 3 s 26</a></p>
      </div>
      <div class="sr" id="stat.176.59"><b>176.59</b> [Repealed, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1953/0/755/#laws.0.83.0">1953 c 755 s 83</a>]</div>
      <div class="section" id="stat.176.591">
        <h1 class="shn">176.591 STATE COMPENSATION REVOLVING FUND.</h1>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.176.591.1">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subdivision 1.<span class="headnote">Establishment.</span></h2>
          <p>To facilitate the discharge by the state of its obligations under this chapter, there is established a revolving fund to be known as the state compensation revolving fund.</p>
          <p>This fund is comprised of the unexpended balance in the fund on July 1, 1935, and the sums which the several departments of the state pay to the fund.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.176.591.2">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 2.<span class="headnote">Commissioner of management and budget as custodian.</span></h2>
          <p>The commissioner of management and budget is custodian of this fund.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.176.591.3">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 3.<span class="headnote">Compensation payments upon request.</span></h2>
          <p>The commissioner of management and budget shall make compensation payments from the fund only as authorized by this chapter upon request of the commissioner of administration.</p>
        </div>
      </div>
      <div class="history" id="stat.176.591.history
			">
        <h2>History: </h2>
        <p class="first"><a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1953/0/755/#laws.0.76.0">1953 c 755 s 76</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1973/0/388/#laws.0.145.0">1973 c 388 s 145</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1987/0/332/#laws.0.108.0">1987 c 332 s 108</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2003/0/112/#laws.2.50.0">2003 c 112 art 2 s 50</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2008/0/204/#laws.0.35.0">2008 c 204 s 35</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2009/0/101/#laws.2.109.0">2009 c 101 art 2 s 109</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2019/1/10/#laws.3.27.0">1Sp2019 c 10 art 3 s 27</a></p>
      </div>
      <div class="sr" id="stat.176.60"><b>176.60</b> [Repealed, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1953/0/755/#laws.0.83.0">1953 c 755 s 83</a>]</div>
      <div class="sr" id="stat.176.601"><b>176.601</b> [Repealed, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1974/0/355/#laws.0.30.0">1974 c 355 s 30</a>]</div>
      <div class="sr" id="stat.176.602"><b>176.602</b> [Repealed, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1987/0/332/#laws.0.117.0">1987 c 332 s 117</a>]</div>
      <div class="section" id="stat.176.603">
        <h1 class="shn">176.603 COST OF ADMINISTERING CHAPTER, PAYMENT.</h1>
        <p>The annual cost to the commissioner of the Department of Administration of administering this chapter in relation to state employees and the necessary expenses which the Department of Administration or the attorney general incurs in investigating, administering, and defending a claim against the state for compensation shall be paid from the state compensation revolving fund.</p>
      </div>
      <div class="history" id="stat.176.603.history
			">
        <h2>History: </h2>
        <p class="first"><a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1974/0/355/#laws.0.32.0">1974 c 355 s 32</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1986/0/461/#laws.0.34.0">1986 c 461 s 34</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1987/0/332/#laws.0.109.0">1987 c 332 s 109</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2008/0/204/#laws.0.36.0">2008 c 204 s 36</a></p>
      </div>
      <div class="sr" id="stat.176.61"><b>176.61</b> [Repealed, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1953/0/755/#laws.0.83.0">1953 c 755 s 83</a>]</div>
      <div class="section" id="stat.176.611">
        <h1 class="shn">176.611 MAINTENANCE OF STATE COMPENSATION REVOLVING FUND.</h1>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.176.611.1">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subdivision 1.<span class="headnote">Generally.</span></h2>
          <p>The state compensation revolving fund shall be maintained as provided in the following subdivisions.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.176.611.2">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 2.<span class="headnote">State departments.</span></h2>
          <p>Every department of the state shall reimburse the fund for money paid for its claims and the costs of administering the revolving fund at such times and in such amounts as the commissioner of administration shall certify has been paid out of the fund on its behalf. The heads of the departments shall anticipate these payments by including them in their budgets. In addition, the commissioner of administration, with the approval of the commissioner of management and budget, may require an agency to make advance payments to the fund sufficient to cover the agency's estimated obligation for a period of at least 60 days. Reimbursements and other money received by the commissioner of administration under this subdivision must be credited to the state compensation revolving fund.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.176.611.2a">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 2a.<span class="headnote">Alternative cost allocation account.</span></h2>
          <p>To reduce long-term costs, minimize impairment to agency operations and budgets, and distribute risk of claims, the commissioner of administration shall maintain a separate account within the state compensation revolving fund. The account shall be used to pay for lump-sum or annuitized settlements, structured claim settlements, and legal, medical, indemnity, or other claim costs that might pose a significant burden for agencies. The commissioner of administration, with the approval of the commissioner of management and budget, may establish criteria and procedures for payment from the account on an agency's behalf. The commissioner of administration may assess agencies on a reimbursement or premium basis from time to time to ensure adequate account reserves. The account consists of appropriations from the general fund, receipts from billings to agencies, and credited investment gains or losses attributable to balances in the account. The State Board of Investment shall invest the assets of the account according to section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/11A.24">11A.24</a>.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.176.611.3">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 3.</h2>
          <p>[Repealed, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1986/0/461/#laws.0.37.0">1986 c 461 s 37</a>]</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.176.611.3a">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 3a.<span class="headnote">Loans.</span></h2>
          <p>To maintain an ongoing balance sufficient to pay sums currently due for benefits and administrative costs, the commissioner of management and budget, upon request of the commissioner of administration, may transfer money from the general fund to the state compensation revolving fund. Before requesting the transfer, the commissioner of administration must decide there is not enough money in the fund for an immediate, necessary expenditure. The amount necessary to make the transfer is appropriated from the general fund to the commissioner of management and budget. The commissioner of administration shall make schedules to repay the transferred money to the general fund. The repayment may not extend beyond five years.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.176.611.4">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 4.</h2>
          <p>[Repealed, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1986/0/461/#laws.0.37.0">1986 c 461 s 37</a>]</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.176.611.5">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 5.</h2>
          <p>[Repealed, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1974/0/355/#laws.0.14.0">1974 c 355 s 14</a>]</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.176.611.6">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 6.</h2>
          <p>[Repealed, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1974/0/355/#laws.0.14.0">1974 c 355 s 14</a>]</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.176.611.6a">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 6a.<span class="headnote">Appropriations constituting fund.</span></h2>
          <p>The revolving fund consists of $3,437,690 appropriated from the general fund and other funds, along with credited investment gains or losses attributable to balances in the account. The State Board of Investment shall invest the fund's assets according to section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/11A.24">11A.24</a>.</p>
        </div>
      </div>
      <div class="history" id="stat.176.611.history
			">
        <h2>History: </h2>
        <p class="first"><a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1953/0/755/#laws.0.78.0">1953 c 755 s 78</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1955/0/744/#laws.0.1.0">1955 c 744 s 1</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1957/0/656/#laws.0.1.0">1957 c 656 s 1</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1963/0/551/#laws.0.1.0">1963 c 551 s 1</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1965/0/57/#laws.0.1.0">1965 c 57 s 1</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1969/0/399/#laws.0.49.0">1969 c 399 s 49</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1971/0/907/#laws.0.1.0">1971 c 907 s 1</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1973/0/388/#laws.0.147.0">1973 c 388 s 147</a>-149; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1974/0/355/#laws.0.15.0">1974 c 355 s 15</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1975/0/204/#laws.0.77.0">1975 c 204 s 77</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1976/0/166/#laws.0.7.0">1976 c 166 s 7</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1979/0/50/#laws.0.19.0">1979 c 50 s 19</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1986/0/461/#laws.0.35.0">1986 c 461 s 35</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1987/0/404/#laws.0.151.0">1987 c 404 s 151</a>-153; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1988/0/667/#laws.0.24.0">1988 c 667 s 24</a>,25; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1994/0/632/#laws.3.52.0">1994 c 632 art 3 s 52</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1997/0/202/#laws.2.41.0">1997 c 202 art 2 s 41</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2000/0/447/#laws.0.22.0">2000 c 447 s 22</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2008/0/204/#laws.0.37.0">2008 c 204 s 37</a>-39; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2009/0/101/#laws.2.109.0">2009 c 101 art 2 s 109</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2017/0/94/#laws.3.9.0">2017 c 94 art 3 s 9</a></p>
      </div>
      <div class="sr" id="stat.176.62"><b>176.62</b> [Repealed, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1953/0/755/#laws.0.83.0">1953 c 755 s 83</a>]</div>
      <div class="sr" id="stat.176.621"><b>176.621</b> [Repealed, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1975/0/61/#laws.0.26.0">1975 c 61 s 26</a>]</div>
      <div class="sr" id="stat.176.63"><b>176.63</b> [Repealed, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1953/0/755/#laws.0.83.0">1953 c 755 s 83</a>]</div>
      <div class="sr" id="stat.176.631"><b>176.631</b> [Repealed, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1975/0/61/#laws.0.26.0">1975 c 61 s 26</a>]</div>
      <div class="sr" id="stat.176.64"><b>176.64</b> [Repealed, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1953/0/755/#laws.0.83.0">1953 c 755 s 83</a>]</div>
      <h1 class="fgh">MISCELLANEOUS</h1>
      <div class="sr" id="stat.176.641"><b>176.641</b> [Repealed, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2014/0/182/#laws.0.8.0">2014 c 182 s 8</a>]</div>
      <div class="section" id="stat.176.645">
        <h1 class="shn">176.645 ADJUSTMENT OF BENEFITS.</h1>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.176.645.1">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subdivision 1.<span class="headnote">Amount.</span></h2>
          <p>For injuries occurring after October 1, 1975, for which benefits are payable under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/176.101">176.101, subdivisions 1, 2 and 4</a>, and section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/176.111#stat.176.111.5">176.111, subdivision 5</a>, the total benefits due the employee or any dependents shall be adjusted in accordance with this section. On October 1, 1981, and thereafter on the anniversary of the date of the employee's injury the total benefits due shall be adjusted by multiplying the total benefits due prior to each adjustment by a fraction, the denominator of which is the statewide average weekly wage for December 31, of the year two years previous to the adjustment and the numerator of which is the statewide average weekly wage for December 31, of the year previous to the adjustment. For injuries occurring after October 1, 1975, all adjustments provided for in this section shall be included in computing any benefit due under this section. Any limitations of amounts due for daily or weekly compensation under this chapter shall not apply to adjustments made under this section. No adjustment increase made on or after October 1, 1977, but prior to October 1, 1992, under this section shall exceed six percent a year; in those instances where the adjustment under the formula of this section would exceed this maximum, the increase shall be deemed to be six percent. No adjustment increase made on or after October 1, 1992, under this section shall exceed four percent a year; in those instances where the adjustment under the formula of this section would exceed this maximum, the increase shall be deemed to be four percent. For injuries occurring on and after October 1, 1995, no adjustment increase made on or after October 1, 1995, shall exceed two percent a year; in those instances where the adjustment under the formula of this section would exceed this maximum, the increase shall be deemed to be two percent. For injuries occurring on and after October 1, 2013, no adjustment increase shall exceed three percent a year. If the adjustment under the formula of this section would exceed three percent, the increase shall be three percent. No adjustment under this section shall be less than zero percent. The Workers' Compensation Advisory Council may consider adjustment or other further increases and make recommendations to the legislature.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.176.645.2">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 2.<span class="headnote">Time of first adjustment.</span></h2>
          <p>For injuries occurring on or after October 1, 1981, the initial adjustment made pursuant to subdivision 1 is deferred until the first anniversary of the date of the injury. For injuries occurring on or after October 1, 1992, the initial adjustment under subdivision 1 is deferred until the second anniversary of the date of the injury. The adjustment made at that time shall be that of the last year only. For injuries occurring on or after October 1, 1995, the initial adjustment under subdivision 1 is deferred until the fourth anniversary of the date of injury. The adjustment at that time shall be that of the last year only. For injuries occurring on or after October 1, 2013, the initial adjustment under subdivision 1 is deferred until the third anniversary of the date of injury. The adjustment made at that time shall be that of the last year only.</p>
        </div>
      </div>
      <div class="history" id="stat.176.645.history
			">
        <h2>History: </h2>
        <p class="first"><a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1975/0/359/#laws.0.20.0">1975 c 359 s 20</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1977/0/342/#laws.0.23.0">1977 c 342 s 23</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1981/0/346/#laws.0.137.0">1981 c 346 s 137</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1992/0/510/#laws.1.12.0">1992 c 510 art 1 s 12</a>,13; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1995/0/231/#laws.1.28.0">1995 c 231 art 1 s 28</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2013/0/70/#laws.2.10.0">2013 c 70 art 2 s 10</a></p>
      </div>
      <div class="sr" id="stat.176.65"><b>176.65</b> [Repealed, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1953/0/755/#laws.0.83.0">1953 c 755 s 83</a>]</div>
      <div class="section" id="stat.176.651">
        <h1 class="shn">176.651 SEVERABILITY.</h1>
        <p>In case for any reason any paragraph or any provision of this chapter shall be questioned in any court of last resort, and shall be held by such court to be unconstitutional or invalid, the same shall not be held to affect any other paragraph or provision thereof.</p>
      </div>
      <div class="history" id="stat.176.651.history
			">
        <h2>History: </h2>
        <p class="first">
          <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1953/0/755/#laws.0.82.0">1953 c 755 s 82</a>
        </p>
      </div>
      <div class="section" id="stat.176.66">
        <h1 class="shn">176.66 OCCUPATIONAL DISEASES; HOW REGARDED.</h1>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.176.66.1">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subdivision 1.<span class="headnote">Disability, disablement.</span></h2>
          <p>The disablement of an employee resulting from an occupational disease shall be regarded as a personal injury within the meaning of the workers' compensation law.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.176.66.2">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 2.</h2>
          <p>[Repealed, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1973/0/643/#laws.0.12.0">1973 c 643 s 12</a>]</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.176.66.3">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 3.</h2>
          <p>[Repealed, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1973/0/643/#laws.0.12.0">1973 c 643 s 12</a>]</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.176.66.4">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 4.</h2>
          <p>[Repealed, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1973/0/643/#laws.0.12.0">1973 c 643 s 12</a>]</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.176.66.5">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 5.</h2>
          <p>[Repealed, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1973/0/643/#laws.0.12.0">1973 c 643 s 12</a>]</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.176.66.6">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 6.</h2>
          <p>[Repealed, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1973/0/643/#laws.0.12.0">1973 c 643 s 12</a>]</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.176.66.7">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 7.</h2>
          <p>[Repealed, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1973/0/643/#laws.0.12.0">1973 c 643 s 12</a>]</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.176.66.8">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 8.</h2>
          <p>[Repealed, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1973/0/643/#laws.0.12.0">1973 c 643 s 12</a>]</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.176.66.9">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 9.</h2>
          <p>[Repealed, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1973/0/643/#laws.0.12.0">1973 c 643 s 12</a>]</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.176.66.10">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 10.<span class="headnote">Multiple employers or insurers; liability.</span></h2>
          <p>The employer liable for the compensation for a personal injury under this chapter is the employer in whose employment the employee was last exposed in a significant way to the hazard of the occupational disease. In the event that the employer who is liable for the compensation had multiple insurers during the employee's term of employment, the insurer who was on the risk during the employee's last significant exposure to the hazard of the occupational disease is the liable party. Where there is a dispute as to which employer is liable under this section, the employer in whose employment the employee is last exposed to the hazard of the occupational disease shall pay benefits pursuant to section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/176.191#stat.176.191.1">176.191, subdivision 1</a>. If this last employer had coverage for workers' compensation liability from more than one insurer during the employment the insurer on the risk during the last period during which the employee was last exposed to the hazard of the occupational disease shall pay benefits as provided under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/176.191#stat.176.191.1">176.191, subdivision 1</a>, whether or not this insurer was on risk during the last significant exposure. The party making payments under this section shall be reimbursed by the party who is subsequently determined to be liable for the occupational disease, including interest at a rate of 12 percent a year. For purposes of this section, a self-insured employer shall be considered to be an insurer and an employer.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.176.66.11">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 11.<span class="headnote">Amount of compensation.</span></h2>
          <p>The compensation for an occupational disease is 66-2/3 percent of the employee's weekly wage on the date of injury subject to a maximum compensation equal to the maximum compensation in effect on the date of last exposure.</p>
        </div>
      </div>
      <div class="history" id="stat.176.66.history
			">
        <h2>History: </h2>
        <p class="first">(<a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/1940A/cite/23">4327</a>) <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1921/0/82/#laws.0.67.0">1921 c 82 s 67</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1939/0/306/#laws.0.0.0">1939 c 306</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1943/0/633/#laws.0.4.0">1943 c 633 s 4</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1947/0/612/#laws.0.1.0">1947 c 612 s 1</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1949/0/500/#laws.0.1.0">1949 c 500 s 1</a>-3; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1955/0/206/#laws.0.2.0">1955 c 206 s 2</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1957/0/834/#laws.0.2.0">1957 c 834 s 2</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1959/0/20/#laws.0.2.0">1959 c 20 s 2</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1963/0/497/#laws.0.2.0">1963 c 497 s 2</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1967/0/905/#laws.0.9.0">1967 c 905 s 9</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1967/1/1/#laws.0.6.0">Ex1967 c 1 s 6</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1973/0/643/#laws.0.11.0">1973 c 643 s 11</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1975/0/359/#laws.0.23.0">1975 c 359 s 23</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1983/0/290/#laws.0.163.0">1983 c 290 s 163</a>,164; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1984/0/432/#laws.2.48.0">1984 c 432 art 2 s 48</a>,49; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1985/0/234/#laws.0.18.0">1985 c 234 s 18</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1995/0/231/#laws.1.29.0">1995 c 231 art 1 s 29</a></p>
      </div>
